{"returncode":0,"stdout":"// prefill_gemm.hpp - Gemm: a validated description of one dense prefill projection  C[M][N] = A[M][K] * W[N][K]^T\n// (row-major activations A, row-major weight rows W, f32 accumulate), with its scratch/workspace accounting and a host\n// reference evaluation used as the oracle for device kernels.\n#pragma once\n#include <cstdint>\n#include <cstddef>\n\nnamespace baloon {\nnamespace pfgemm {\n\nenum class Elem : uint8_t { F32 = 0, F16 = 1, BF16 = 2 };\n\ninline uint64_t elem_bytes(Elem e) { return e == Elem::F32 ? 4u : 2u; }\n\n// Tile geometry: M, N rounded up to multiples of kTileM / kTileN, K to kTileK (zero padded).\nconstexpr uint64_t kTileM = 64, kTileN = 64, kTileK = 32;\n\ninline uint64_t round_up(uint64_t v, uint64_t a) {\n    const uint64_t r = v % a;\n    return r == 0 ? v : v + (a - r);\n}\ninline bool mul_overflows(uint64_t a, uint64_t b) { return a != 0 && b > UINT64_MAX / a; }\ninline bool mul_checked(uint64_t a, uint64_t b, uint64_t* out) {\n    if (mul_overflows(a, b)) return false;\n    *out = a * b;\n    return true;\n}\n\nstruct Gemm {\n    uint64_t m = 0, n = 0, k = 0;          // tokens, output rows, reduction width\n    Elem a_type = Elem::F32, w_type = Elem::F32;\n\n    // Every quantity the other members compute (padded extents, A/W/scratch/output/workspace byte counts, flops) must be\n    // representable in 64 bits, so no accessor below can wrap.\n    bool valid() const {\n        if (m == 0 || n == 0 || k == 0) return false;\n        const uint64_t pm = round_up(m, kTileM), pn = round_up(n, kTileN), pk = round_up(k, kTileK);\n        if (pm < m || pn < n || pk < k) return false;\n        uint64_t t = 0;\n        if (!mul_checked(pm, pk, &t) || !mul_checked(t, elem_bytes(a_type), &t)) return false;   // padded A bytes\n        if (!mul_checked(pn, pk, &t) || !mul_checked(t, elem_bytes(w_type), &t)) return false;   // padded W bytes\n        if (a_type != w_type && (!mul_checked(pm, pk, &t) || !mul_checked(t, elem_bytes(w_type), &t))) return false;  // scratch bytes\n        uint64_t mn = 0;\n        if (!mul_checked(m, n, &mn) || !mul_checked(mn, 4, &t)) return false;                    // f32 output bytes\n        if (!mul_checked(mn, k, &t) || !mul_checked(t, 2, &t)) return false;                     // flops\n        uint64_t tiles = 0;\n        if (!mul_checked(pm / kTileM, pn / kTileN, &tiles) || !mul_checked(tiles, kTileM * kTileN * 4ull, &t)) return false;  // workspace\n        return true;\n    }\n    uint64_t flops() const { return 2ull * m * n * k; }                       // multiply + add per term\n    uint64_t out_bytes() const { return m * n * 4ull; }                        // f32 output\n    // Scratch holding A converted to the weight type when they differ, padded to tiles; 0 when no conversion is needed.\n    uint64_t scratch_bytes() const {\n        if (a_type == w_type) return 0;\n        return round_up(m, kTileM) * round_up(k, kTileK) * elem_bytes(w_type);\n    }\n    // Workspace: one f32 partial tile per output tile column block (split-k staging).\n    uint64_t workspace_bytes() const { return (round_up(m, kTileM) / kTileM) * (round_up(n, kTileN) / kTileN) * kTileM * kTileN * 4ull; }\n    bool fits(uint64_t scratch_cap, uint64_t ws_cap) const {\n        return valid() && scratch_bytes() <= scratch_cap && workspace_bytes() <= ws_cap;\n    }\n\n    // Host reference: out[t*n + r] = sum_c a[t*k + c] * w[r*k + c], summed in ascending c order in f32.\n    // Returns false (nothing written) when the description is invalid or a pointer is null.\n    bool reference(const float* a, const float* w, float* out) const {\n        if (!valid() || !a || !w || !out) return false;\n        for (uint64_t t = 0; t < m; ++t)\n            for (uint64_t r = 0; r < n; ++r) {\n                float acc = 0.f;\n                for (uint64_t c = 0; c < k; ++c) acc += a[t * k + c] * w[r * k + c];\n                out[t * n + r] = acc;\n            }\n        return true;\n    }\n};\n\n// Splits m tokens into chunks of at most `chunk`; the chunk spans tile every token exactly once.\ninline uint64_t chunk_count(uint64_t m, uint64_t chunk) { return chunk == 0 ? 0 : m / chunk + (m % chunk != 0); }\n\n}  // namespace pfgemm\n}  // namespace baloon\nkernels.cu\nprefill_host.cpp\n// Invariants of include/baloon/prefill_gemm.hpp.\n#include <cstdint>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <vector>\n#include \"baloon/prefill_gemm.hpp\"\n#include \"check.hpp\"\nusing namespace baloon::pfgemm;\n\nstatic void test_validity() {\n    Gemm g; CHECK(!g.valid());\n    g.m = 1; g.n = 1; g.k = 1; CHECK(g.valid());\n    g.k = 0; CHECK(!g.valid());\n    g.m = UINT64_MAX; g.n = 2; g.k = 1; CHECK(!g.valid());\n    g.m = 1ull << 40; g.n = 1ull << 40; g.k = 1; CHECK(!g.valid());   // m*n overflows\n}\nstatic Gemm mk(uint64_t m, uint64_t n, uint64_t k, Elem a = Elem::F32, Elem w = Elem::F32) {\n    Gemm g; g.m = m; g.n = n; g.k = k; g.a_type = a; g.w_type = w; return g;\n}\n// valid() must reject every description for which any accessor would wrap past 2^64, and accept the boundary cases below it.\nstatic void test_overflow_contract() {\n    CHECK(!mk(1, 1, 1ull << 62).valid());                       // padded A bytes: k*4 wraps to 0\n    CHECK(!mk(1, 1, 1ull << 62, Elem::F16, Elem::F16).valid());\n    CHECK(!mk(1, 1, 1ull << 57).valid());                       // 64 * 2^57 * 4 wraps\n    CHECK(!mk(1, 1, 1ull << 57, Elem::F16, Elem::F32).valid()); // a=F16 / w=F32 scratch + W bytes wrap\n    CHECK(!mk(1, 1, 1ull << 61, Elem::F16, Elem::F32).valid());\n    CHECK(!mk(1, 1, UINT64_MAX).valid());                       // round_up wraps\n    CHECK(!mk(1ull << 31, 1ull << 31, 1).valid());              // m*n*4 wraps\n    CHECK(!mk(1ull << 20, 1ull << 20, 1ull << 23).valid());     // flops wrap, bytes fine\n    CHECK(!mk((1ull << 31) - 1, (1ull << 31) - 1, 1).valid());  // workspace (padded) wraps, m*n*4 does not\n    CHECK(!mk(1ull << 58, 1, 1, Elem::F16, Elem::BF16).valid());// padded A bytes wrap via pm*pk\n    CHECK(!mk(1ull << 40, 1, 1ull << 22).valid());              // only padded A bytes wrap (flops, W, out, ws fit)\n    CHECK(!mk(1, 1ull << 40, 1ull << 22).valid());              // only padded W bytes wrap\n    // Scratch-only wrap: a=F16 (A bytes 2^63 fit), w=F32 (scratch 2^40*2^22*4 = 2^64 wraps to 0); W, out, flops, ws all fit.\n    CHECK(!mk(1ull << 40, 1, 1ull << 22, Elem::F16, Elem::F32).valid());\n    CHECK(!mk(1ull << 40, 1, 1ull << 22, Elem::BF16, Elem::F32).valid());\n    CHECK(mk(1ull << 40, 1, 1ull << 21, Elem::F16, Elem::F32).valid());   // half of that fits\n    CHECK(mk(1ull << 40, 1, 1ull << 22, Elem::F16, Elem::F16).valid());   // no conversion -> no scratch, A bytes 2^63 fit\n    CHECK(mk(1ull << 40, 1, 1ull << 22, Elem::F32, Elem::F16).valid() == false);  // A bytes 2^64 wrap\n    CHECK(mk(1ull << 40, 1, 1ull << 21).valid());               // half of that is fine\n    CHECK(mk(1, 1ull << 40, 1ull << 21).valid());\n    // boundary: just below the limits is accepted and its accessors do not wrap\n    Gemm ok = mk(1ull << 20, 1ull << 20, 1ull << 22);\n    CHECK(ok.valid());\n    CHECK(ok.flops() == 2ull * (1ull << 20) * (1ull << 20) * (1ull << 22));\n    CHECK(ok.out_bytes() == (1ull << 40) * 4);\n    Gemm sc = mk(64, 64, 1ull << 20, Elem::F16, Elem::F32);\n    CHECK(sc.valid() && sc.scratch_bytes() == 64ull * (1ull << 20) * 4);\n    Gemm ws = mk(1ull << 30, 1ull << 30, 1);\n    CHECK(ws.valid() && ws.workspace_bytes() == (1ull << 60) * 4);\n}\nstatic void test_accounting() {\n    Gemm g; g.m = 100; g.n = 70; g.k = 33;\n    CHECK(g.flops() == 2ull * 100 * 70 * 33);\n    CHECK(g.out_bytes() == 100ull * 70 * 4);\n    CHECK(g.scratch_bytes() == 0);\n    g.w_type = Elem::BF16;\n    CHECK(g.scratch_bytes() == 128ull * 64 * 2);\n    CHECK(g.workspace_bytes() == 2ull * 2 * 64 * 64 * 4);\n    CHECK(g.fits(128 * 64 * 2, 2 * 2 * 64 * 64 * 4));\n    CHECK(!g.fits(128 * 64 * 2 - 1, 1ull << \\\"$ROOT\\\"\\nOUT=\\\"${BALOON_BUILD_DIR:-/tmp/baloon_unit_pcd0_build}\\\"\\n# Start from an empty output dir: the only binaries left here are this unit's (AVX-512 objects included), so nothing EXIT\\nCOREX=/usr/local/corex\\nCLANG=\\\"$COREX/bin/clang++\\\"\\nHOSTCXX=\\\"\\\"\\nfor c in \\\"$CLANG\\\" g++ clang++ c++; do\\n  if [ -x \\\"$c\\\" ] || command -v \\\"$c\\\" >/dev/null 2>&1; sources (the reference built q2_avx2.cpp with -mavx2 -mf16c -mfma).\\n# ISA512: the AVX-512 sources of earlier units keep their own flags regardless of EINSTEINOS_ISA_FLAGS.\\nISA=\\\"${EINSTEINOS_ISA_FLAGS:--mavx2 -mf16c -mfma}\\\"\\nISA512=\\\"-mavx512bw -mavx512dq -mavx512f -mavx512vbmi -mavx512vl -mavx512vnni -mf16c -mfma\\\"\\nHFLAGS=\\\"-std=c++17 -O2 -Wall -Wextra -ffp-contract=off -pthread -Iinclude\\\"\\nCUFLAGS=\\\"-x cuda --cuda-gpu-arch=ivcore10 --cuda-path=$COREX -std=c++17 -D__ILUVATAR__ -I$COREX/include -Isrc -Isrc/cu -Itests -Itests/pk_unit3 -Iinclude -O2\\\"\\nHAVE_CLANG=0; [ -x \\\"$CLANG\\\" ] && HAVE_CLANG=1\\n\\n# ---- headers: each must compile on its own (the CUDA ones as CUDA) ------------------------------------------------------\\nfor h in include/baloon/*.hpp; do\\n  echo \\\"[header] $h\\\"\\n  if grep -q 'cuda_runtime' \\\"$h\\\"; then\\n    [ \\\"$HAVE_CLANG\\\" = 1 ] || { echo \\\"corex toolchain missing at $CLANG: cannot check CUDA header $h\\\"; exit 2; }\\n    printf '#include \\\"%s\\\"\\\\nint main(){return 0;}\\\\n' \\\"baloon/$(basename \\\"$h\\\")\\\" > \\\"$OUT/hdr_check.cu\\\"\\n    \\\"$CLANG\\\" $CUFLAGS -fsyntax-only \\\"$OUT/hdr_check.cu\\\"\\n  else\\n    printf '#include \\\"%s\\\"\\\\nint main(){return 0;}\\\\n' \\\"baloon/$(basename \\\"$h\\\")\\\" > \\\"$OUT/hdr_check.cpp\\\"\\n    $HOSTCXX $HFLAGS -fsyntax-only \\\"$OUT/hdr_check.cpp\\\"\\n  fi\\ndone\\n\\n# ---- library objects ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\\nfor f in src/core/*.cpp src/prefill/*.cpp; do\\n  [ -f \\\"$f\\\" ] || continue\\n  echo \\\"$ROOT\\\"\\nOUT=\\\"${BALOON_TEST_DIR:-/tmp/baloon_unit_pcd0_test}\\\"\\nrm -rf \\\"$OUT\\\"; mkdir -p \\\"$OUT\\\"\\nCOREX=/usr/local/corex\\nCLANG=\\\"$COREX/bin/clang++\\\"\\n[ -x \\\"$CLANG\\\" ] || { echo \\\"corex toolchain missing at $CLANG\\\"; exit 2; }\\nCUFLAGS=\\\"-x cuda --cuda-gpu-arch=ivcore10 --cuda-path=$COREX -std=c++17 -D__ILUVATAR__ -I$COREX/include -Isrc -O2\\\"\\nrc=0\\n\\necho \\\"== port table ==\\\"\\npython3 - .einsteinOS/port/pcd027bc02f.tsv <<'PY' || rc=1\\nimport re, sys\\nbad = 0; n = 0; seen = set()\\nfor line in open(sys.argv[1]):\\n    f = line.rstrip(\\\"\\\\r\\\\n\\\").split(\\\"\\\\t\\\")\\n    if len(f) < 4 or not f[0]: continue\\n    n += 1; seen.add((f[0], f[1]))\\n    try: t = open(f[2], errors=\\\"ignore\\\").read()\\n    except OSError: print(\\\"port: missing file\\\", f[2]); bad += 1; continue\\n    esc = re.escape(f[3])\\n    pat = (r'(struct|class|enum)\\\\s+(class\\\\s+)?' + esc + r'\\\\b|using\\\\s+' + esc + \\\\\\\"$ROOT\\\\\\\"\\\\nOUT=\\\\\\\"${BALOON_BUILD_DIR:-/tmp/baloon_unit_p1bc_build}\\\\\\\"\\\\n# Start from an empty output dir: the only binaries left here are this unit's (AVX-512 objects included), so nothing stale\\\\n# or unrelated from earlier units crowds them out when the build outputs are inspected.\\\\nrm -rf \\\\\\\"$OUT\\\\\\\"; mkdir -p \\\\\\\"$OUT\\\\\\\"\\\\nSCR=\\\\\\\"$(mktemp -d)\\\\\\\"; trap 'rm -rf \\\\\\\"$SCR\\\\\\\"' EXIT\\\\nCOREX=/usr/local/corex\\\\nCLANG=\\\\\\\"$COREX/bin/clang++\\\\\\\"\\\\nHOSTCXX=\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nfor c in \\\\\\\"$CLANG\\\\\\\" g++ clang++ c++; do\\\\n  if [ -x \\\\\\\"$c\\\\\\\" ] || command -v \\\\\\\"$c\\\\\\\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then HOSTCXX=\\\\\\\"$c\\\\\\\"; break; fi\\\\ndone\\\\n[ -n \\\\\\\"$HOSTCXX\\\\\\\" ] || { echo \\\\\\\"no C++ compiler found\\\\\\\"; exit 2; }\\\\necho \\\\\\\"host compiler: $HOSTCXX\\\\\\\"\\\\n# ISA: flags for this unit's AVX2 sources (the reference built q2_avx2.cpp with -mavx2 -mf16c -mfma).\\\\n# ISA512: the AVX-512 sources of --cuda-gpu-arch=ivcore10 --cuda-path=$COREX -std=c++17 -D__ILUVATAR__ -I$COREX/include -Isrc -Isrc/cu -Itests -Itests/pk_unit3 -Iinclude -O2\\\\\\\"\\\\nHAVE_CLANG=0; [ -x \\\\\\\"$CLANG\\\\\\\" ] && The actual compiler is `/usr/local/corex/bin/clang-16`. Always use `clang++` directly with `-x cuda`.\\\\n\\\\n### Known limitations (ivcore10)\\\\n\\\\n- `__threadfence_system()` — NOT supported, use `__threadfence()` (device-level) or host barriers\\\\n- CUDA IPC (`cudaIpcGetMemHandle`) — NOT supported on BI-V100, use `cudaMemcpyPeer` instead\\\\n- Max CUDA compat: 10.2 — no cooperative_groups advanced features\\\\n- warpSize: 64 (not 32 like src/ix_compat.h src/ix_bf16_gemv.cu src/ix_shared_expert.cu src/ix_moe_combine.cu\\n{\\\"returncode\\\":0,\\\"stdout\\\":\\\"// ix_compat.h — Iluvatar BI-V100 compatibility layer\\\\n// Derived from Ref include/ref/hip_compat/intrinsics.hpp (163 lines, verified)\\\\n#pragma once\\\\n#ifndef __ILUVATAR__\\\\n#error \\\\\\\"ix_compat.h is for Iluvatar BI-V100 only\\\\\\\"\\\\n#endif\\\\n\\\\n#include <cstdint>\\\\n#include <cstring>   // memcpy (ix_bf16_to_f32)\\\\n#include <cmath>     // fmaxf, exp\\\\n\\\\n#define IX_WARP_SIZE 64\\\\n\\\\n// ========== Warp-level shuffles (width=64, no _sync needed) ==========\\\\n#define ix_shfl_xor(v, off)       __shfl_xor(v, off, 64)\\\\n#define ix_shfl_down(v, delta)    __shfl_down(v, delta, 64)\\\\n#define ix_shfl_up(v, delta)      __shfl_up(v, delta, 64)\\\\n#define ix_shfl(v, lane)          __shfl(v, lane, 64)\\\\n\\\\n// ========== 64-lane butterfly reductions ==========\\\\n// From Ref native_mmvq.cu:2363-2365 (verified: for off = 32; off > 0; off >>= 1)\\\\ntemplate<typename T>\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ T ix_warp_reduce_sum(T v) {\\\\n    #pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int off = 32; off > 0; off >>= 1)\\\\n        v += __shfl_xor(v, off, 64);\\\\n    return v;\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\ntemplate<typename T>\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ T ix_warp_reduce_max(T v) {\\\\n    #pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int off = 32; off > 0; off >>= 1)\\\\n        v = fmaxf(v, __shfl_xor(v, off, 64));\\\\n    return v;\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// Double-precision warp reduction (from shared_expert.cu:107-110, rewritten for wave64)\\\\n// Original uses warp32: __shfl_down_sync(0xFFFFFFFFu, v, off) with off=16..1\\\\n// ivcore10: __shfl_down(v, off, 64) with off=32..1\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ double ix_warp_sum_d(double v) {\\\\n    #pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int off = 32; off > 0; off >>= 1)\\\\n        v += __shfl_down(v, off, 64);\\\\n    return __shfl(v, 0, 64);\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// ========== Half-wave (32-lane) helpers ==========\\\\n// Ref's CUDA kernels written for 32-lane warps (fused_gr.cu: lane=t&31, warp=t>>5, xor tree o=16..1) stay correct on\\\\n// wave64 ONLY if every shuffle is confined to the aligned 32-lane half. Always pass width=32 explicitly: an xor offset <32\\\\n// never leaves the half by itself, but an ABSOLUTE source lane (__shfl(v, src)) with the default width would read the lower\\\\n// half from the upper one. See FUSED_GR_WAVE64_AUDIT.md.\\\\n#define ix_shfl_xor32(v, off)     __shfl_xor(v, off, 32)\\\\n#define ix_shfl32(v, lane)        __shfl(v, lane, 32)\\\\ntemplate<typename T>\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ T ix_halfwave_reduce_sum(T v) {\\\\n    #pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int off = 16; off > 0; off >>= 1) v += __shfl_xor(v, off, 32);\\\\n    return v;\\\\n}\\\\ntemplate<typename T>\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ T ix_halfwave_reduce_max(T v) {\\\\n    #pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int off = 16; off > 0; off >>= 1) v = fmaxf(v, __shfl_xor(v, off, 32));\\\\n    return v;\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// ========== Threadfence — NEVER use __threadfence_system() ==========\\\\n#define ix_threadfence()          __threadfence()\\\\n\\\\n// __nanosleep — NOT on ivcore10 (Ref intrinsics.hpp:161)\\\\n#define ix_nanosleep(ns)          __threadfence()\\\\n\\\\n// ========== Lane/warp helpers ==========\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ int ix_lane_id() { return threadIdx.x & 63; }\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ int ix_warp_id() { return threadIdx.x >> 6; }\\\\n\\\\n// ========== Ballot (64-bit on warp64) ==========\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ unsigned long long ix_ballot(int pred) {\\\\n    return __ballot(pred);\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// ========== dp4a — portable signed dot4 (Ref intrinsics.hpp:46-53 fallback) ==========\\\\n// The portable path is used: ivcore10 has no __builtin_amdgcn_sudot4/sdot4.\\\\n// Verified: exact same algorithm as Ref's #else branch at lines 46-53.\\\\nnamespace ix_compat_detail {\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ int signed_byte(uint32_t word, int lane) {\\\\n    const unsigned value = (word >> (lane * 8)) & 0xffu;\\\\n    return value < 0x80u ? static_cast<int>(value) : static_cast<int>(value) - 0x100;\\\\n}\\\\n}  // namespace ix_compat_detail\\\\n\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ int ix_dp4a(int a, int b, int c) {\\\\n    const uint32_t ua = static_cast<uint32_t>(a);\\\\n    const uint32_t ub = static_cast<uint32_t>(b);\\\\n    uint32_t sum = static_cast<uint32_t>(c);\\\\n    #pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int lane = 0; lane < 4; ++lane)\\\\n        sum += static_cast<uint32_t>(\\\\n            ix_compat_detail::signed_byte(ua, lane) * ix_compat_detail::signed_byte(ub, lane));\\\\n    return static_cast<int>(sum);\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// ========== byte_perm — software emulation ==========\\\\n// Ref intrinsics.hpp:60-63 uses __builtin_amdgcn_perm; ivcore10 has no v_perm_b32.\\\\n// CUDA semantics: result byte i = byte (s.nibble[i] & 7) of the pair {y:x}, x the low word.\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ uint32_t ix_byte_perm(uint32_t x, uint32_t y, uint32_t s) {\\\\n    const uint32_t pair[2] = {x, y};\\\\n    const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(pair);\\\\n    uint32_t result = 0;\\\\n    #pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {\\\\n        const int sel = (s >> (i * 4)) & 0x7;\\\\n        result |= static_cast<uint32_t>(bytes[sel]) << (i * 8);\\\\n    }\\\\n    return result;\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// ========== SWAR packed-byte ops (Ref intrinsics.hpp:68-96, verified) ==========\\\\n// Used by Q3_K/Q6_K dots and i-quant sign expansion. Pure arithmetic, no intrinsic.\\\\nnamespace ix_swar {\\\\nconstexpr uint32_t kHigh = 0x80808080u;\\\\n\\\\n// Packed unsigned byte subtract, wraps per lane (Ref L72-76)\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ int vsub4(int a, int b) {\\\\n    const uint32_t ua = static_cast<uint32_t>(a);\\\\n    const uint32_t ub = static_cast<uint32_t>(b);\\\\n    return static_cast<int>(((ua | kHigh) - (ub & ~kHigh)) ^ ((ua ^ ~ub) & kHigh));\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// Packed signed byte saturating subtract (Ref L80-88)\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ int vsubss4(int a, int b) {\\\\n    const uint32_t ua = static_cast<uint32_t>(a);\\\\n    const uint32_t ub = static_cast<uint32_t>(b);\\\\n    const uint32_t d = static_cast<uint32_t>(vsub4(a, b));\\\\n    const uint32_t overflow = (ua ^ ub) & (ua ^ d) & kHigh;\\\\n    const uint32_t mask = (overflow >> 7) * 0xffu;\\\\n    const uint32_t bound = 0x7f7f7f7fu + ((ua & kHigh) >> 7);\\\\n    return static_cast<int>((d & ~mask) | (bound & mask));\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// Four-byte lane compare: 0xff for unequal, 0 for equal (Ref L92-96)\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ int vcmpne4(int a, int b) {\\\\n    const uint32_t t = static_cast<uint32_t>(a) ^ static_cast<uint32_t>(b);\\\\n    const uint32_t nonzero = (((t & ~kHigh) + ~kHigh) | t) & kHigh;\\\\n    return static_cast<int>((nonzero >> 7) * 0xffu);\\\\n}\\\\n}  // namespace ix_swar\\\\n\\\\n// ========== Macro aliases (matching Ref's #define at L146-161) ==========\\\\n#define __dp4a(a, b, c)            ix_dp4a((a), (b), (c))\\\\n#define __byte_perm(x, y, s)      ix_byte_perm((x), (y), (s))\\\\n#define __vsub4(a, b)             ix_swar::vsub4((a), (b))\\\\n#define __vsubss4(a, b)           ix_swar::vsubss4((a), (b))\\\\n#define __vcmpne4(a, b)           ix_swar::vcmpne4((a), (b))\\\\n\\\\n// BF16 conversion helpers (used by shared_expert.cu:117, bf16_gemv.cu:25)\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ float ix_bf16_to_f32(uint16_t bf16) {\\\\n    uint32_t bits = static_cast<uint32_t>(bf16) << 16;\\\\n    float f;\\\\n    memcpy(&f, &bits, 4);\\\\n    return f;\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// ix_bf16_gemv.cu - Phase 1 #8: BF16 GEMV for ivcore10 (wave64).\\\\n// Port of Ref src/kernels/cuda/bf16_gemv.cu (HEAD 46c152a1): naive / warp-per-row / split / pair kernels.\\\\n// ivcore10 changes (v9 Part H): ALL warp32 patterns -> wave64: lane&63, tid>>6, stride 64, shfl_down off=32..1 width 64,\\\\n// 4 waves per 256-thread block. Weights/activations stay BF16 *bits* (ix_bf16_to_f32 is an exact <<16, no FP16 round trip);\\\\n// accumulation is float, as in Ref.\\\\n// NOT bitwise equal to Ref's warp32 result: lane stride and tree shape change the float summation order. Accept = within\\\\n// float tolerance of a double reference. What IS preserved: pair kernel == standalone warp kernel, bitwise (same loop/order).\\\\n// threads_per_row==64 selects warp-per-row (Ref used 32). tpr=32 goes through the shared-memory tree and is correct but slow.\\\\n// STATUS: NOT yet compiled or run on BI-V100.\\\\n#pragma once\\\\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\\\\n#include <cstdint>\\\\n#include \\\\\\\"ix_compat.h\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nnamespace ix_bf {\\\\nconstexpr int THREADS = 256;\\\\nconstexpr int WAVES = THREADS / IX_WARP_SIZE;   // 4\\\\n\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ float wave_sum_f(float v) {\\\\n#pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int off = 32; off > 0; off >>= 1) v += __shfl_down(v, off, 64);\\\\n    return v;                                   // valid in lane 0 only (as in Ref)\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n__global__ void bf16_gemv_naive_kernel(const uint16_t* __restrict__ x, const uint16_t* __restrict__ w,\\\\n                                       float* __restrict__ y, long long n_in, long long n_out) {\\\\n    const long long o = (long long) blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\\\\n    if (o >= n_out) return;\\\\n    const uint16_t* row = w + o * n_in;\\\\n    float acc = 0.0f;\\\\n    for (long long i = 0; i < n_in; ++i) acc += ix_bf16_to_f32(x[i]) * ix_bf16_to_f32(row[i]);\\\\n    y[o] = acc;\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n__global__ void bf16_gemv_warp_kernel(const uint16_t* __restrict__ x, const uint16_t* __restrict__ w,\\\\n                                      float* __restrict__ y, long long n_in, long long n_out) {\\\\n    const long long o = (long long) blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x >> 6) + (threadIdx.x >> 6);\\\\n    if (o >= n_out) return;                       // whole wave exits together (o is wave-uniform)\\\\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 63;\\\\n    const uint16_t* row = w + o * n_in;\\\\n    float acc = 0.0f;\\\\n    for (long long i = lane; i < n_in; i += 64) acc += ix_bf16_to_f32(x[i]) * ix_bf16_to_f32(row[i]);\\\\n    acc = wave_sum_f(acc);\\\\n    if (lane == 0) y[o] = acc;\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// tpr threads per row, shared-memory tree; tpr must be a power of two (<= 1024). Unchanged from Ref (no warp ops).\\\\n__global__ void bf16_gemv_split_kernel(const uint16_t* __restrict__ x, const uint16_t* __restrict__ w,\\\\n                                       float* __restrict__ y, long long n_in, long long n_out, int tpr) {\\\\n    extern __shared__ float scratch[];\\\\n    const long long o = blockIdx.x;\\\\n    if (o >= n_out) return;\\\\n    const int t = threadIdx.x;\\\\n    const uint16_t* row = w + o * n_in;\\\\n    float acc = 0.0f;\\\\n    for (long long i = t; i < n_in; i += tpr) acc += ix_bf16_to_f32(x[i]) * ix_bf16_to_f32(row[i]);\\\\n    scratch[t] = acc;\\\\n    __syncthreads();\\\\n    for (int off = tpr >> 1; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\\\\n        if (t < off) scratch[t] += scratch[t + off];\\\\n        __syncthreads();\\\\n    }\\\\n    if (t == 0) y[o] = scratch[0];\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n__global__ void bf16_gemv_pair_warp_kernel(const uint16_t* __restrict__ x, const uint16_t* __restrict__ w1,\\\\n                                           float* __restrict__ y1, long long n_out1,\\\\n                                           const uint16_t* __restrict__ w2, float* __restrict__ y2,\\\\n                                           long long n_out2, long long n_in) {\\\\n    const long long o = (long long) blockIdx.x * (blockDim.x >> 6) + (threadIdx.x >> 6);\\\\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x & 63;\\\\n    const long long n_out = n_out1 > n_out2 ? n_out1 : n_out2;\\\\n    if (o >= n_out) return;\\\\n    const int run1 = o < n_out1, run2 = o < n_out2;\\\\n    const uint16_t* r1 = w1 + o * n_in;\\\\n    const uint16_t* r2 = w2 + o * n_in;\\\\n    float acc1 = 0.0f, acc2 = 0.0f;\\\\n    for (long long i = lane; i < n_in; i += 64) {\\\\n        const float xv = ix_bf16_to_f32(x[i]);\\\\n        if (run1) acc1 += xv * ix_bf16_to_f32(r1[i]);\\\\n        if (run2) acc2 += xv * ix_bf16_to_f32(r2[i]);\\\\n    }\\\\n    // run1/run2 are wave-uniform (depend only on o), so the shuffles below are never divergent.\\\\n    if (run1) { const float a = wave_sum_f(acc1); if (lane == 0) y1[o] = a; }\\\\n    if (run2) { const float a = wave_sum_f(acc2); if (lane == 0) y2[o] = a; }\\\\n}\\\\n}  // namespace ix_bf\\\\n\\\\n// Host launchers return cudaError_t (Ref's exit(1) on error is not appropriate for a library).\\\\ninline cudaError_t ix_bf16_gemv(const uint16_t* x, const uint16_t* w, float* y, long long n_in, long long n_out,\\\\n                                cudaStream_t s = 0) {\\\\n    if (n_in <= 0 || n_out <= 0) return cudaErrorInvalidValue;\\\\n    if (n_out >= 64) {   // threshold carried over from Ref (48 SMs there); retune once BI-V100 SM count is known\\\\n        ix_bf::bf16_gemv_warp_kernel<<<(unsigned) ((n_out + ix_bf::WAVES - 1) / ix_bf::WAVES), ix_bf::THREADS, 0, s>>>(\\\\n            x, w, y, n_in, n_out);\\\\n    } else {\\\\n        ix_bf::bf16_gemv_naive_kernel<<<(unsigned) ((n_out + ix_bf::THREADS - 1) / ix_bf::THREADS), ix_bf::THREADS, 0, s>>>(\\\\n            x, w, y, n_in, n_out);\\\\n    }\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\ninline cudaError_t ix_bf16_gemv_pair(const uint16_t* x, const uint16_t* w1, float* y1, long long n_out1,\\\\n                                     const uint16_t* w2, float* y2, long long n_out2, long long n_in,\\\\n                                     cudaStream_t s = 0) {\\\\n    if (n_in <= 0 || (n_out1 <= 0 && n_out2 <= 0)) return cudaErrorInvalidValue;\\\\n    const long long n_out = n_out1 > n_out2 ? n_out1 : n_out2;\\\\n    ix_bf::bf16_gemv_pair_warp_kernel<<<(unsigned) ((n_out + ix_bf::WAVES - 1) / ix_bf::WAVES), ix_bf::THREADS, 0, s>>>(\\\\n        x, w1, y1, n_out1, w2, y2, n_out2, n_in);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\ninline cudaError_t ix_bf16_gemv_split(const uint16_t* x, const uint16_t* w, float* y, long long n_in, long long n_out,\\\\n                                      int threads_per_row, cudaStream_t s = 0) {\\\\n    if (n_in <= 0 || n_out <= 0) return cudaErrorInvalidValue;\\\\n    if (threads_per_row == 64) {   // warp-per-row path (Ref: 32)\\\\n        ix_bf::bf16_gemv_warp_kernel<<<(unsigned) ((n_out + ix_bf::WAVES - 1) / ix_bf::WAVES), ix_bf::THREADS, 0, s>>>(\\\\n            x, w, y, n_in, n_out);\\\\n        return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n    }\\\\n    if (threads_per_row <= 0 || threads_per_row > 1024 || (threads_per_row & (threads_per_row - 1)) != 0)\\\\n        return cudaErrorInvalidValue;\\\\n    ix_bf::bf16_gemv_split_kernel<<<(unsigned) n_out, threads_per_row, (size_t) threads_per_row * sizeof(float), s>>>(\\\\n        x, w, y, n_in, n_out, threads_per_row);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n}\\\\n// ix_shared_expert.cu - Phase 1 #4: shared-expert helpers for ivcore10 (wave64).\\\\n// Port of Ref src/kernels/cuda/shared_expert.cu (HEAD 46c152a1): swiglu_kernel, scalar_gate_kernel, scale_kernel.\\\\n// ivcore10 changes (v9 Part H): warp_sum_d 32->64 lanes (ix_warp_sum_d), lane&63, warp>>6, scratch[4] for <<<1,256>>>.\\\\n// Semantics kept verbatim: SiLU goes on the GATE tensor; the gate is ONE scalar per token = sigmoid(dot(x, w_shexp)),\\\\n// both operands BF16, double accumulator; the shared output is added plain by ix_moe_combine (not router-weighted).\\\\n// STATUS: NOT yet compiled or run on BI-V100.\\\\n#pragma once\\\\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\\\\n#include <cstdint>\\\\n#include \\\\\\\"ix_compat.h\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nnamespace ix_se {\\\\nconstexpr int GATE_THREADS = 256;                       // 4 wavefronts of 64\\\\nconstexpr int GATE_WAVES   = GATE_THREADS / IX_WARP_SIZE;\\\\n\\\\n// silu(g) * up, silu evaluated in DOUBLE then cast (reference is float64), multiply in float - Ref L55-61.\\\\n__global__ void swiglu_kernel(const float* __restrict__ gate, const float* __restrict__ up,\\\\n                              float* __restrict__ out, int n) {\\\\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\\\\n    if (i >= n) return;\\\\n    const double x = (double) gate[i];\\\\n    out[i] = (float) (x / (1.0 + exp(-x))) * up[i];\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// sigmoid(dot(x_bf16, w_bf16)): launch <<<1, GATE_THREADS>>> only (scratch is sized for it).\\\\n__global__ void scalar_gate_kernel(const uint16_t* __restrict__ x_bf16, const uint16_t* __restrict__ w_bf16,\\\\n                                   float* __restrict__ out, int n_embd) {\\\\n    __shared__ double scratch[GATE_WAVES];\\\\n    double acc = 0.0;\\\\n    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < n_embd; i += blockDim.x)\\\\n        acc += (double) ix_bf16_to_f32(x_bf16[i]) * (double) ix_bf16_to_f32(w_bf16[i]);\\\\n    const int lane = ix_lane_id(), wave = ix_warp_id();\\\\n    acc = ix_warp_sum_d(acc);                            // every lane of the wave now holds the wave sum\\\\n    if (lane == 0) scratch[wave] = acc;\\\\n    __syncthreads();\\\\n    if (wave == 0) {\\\\n        acc = (threadIdx.x < GATE_WAVES) ? scratch[threadIdx.x] : 0.0;\\\\n        acc = ix_warp_sum_d(acc);                        // lanes >= GATE_WAVES contribute 0\\\\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) out[0] = (float) (1.0 / (1.0 + exp(-acc)));\\\\n    }\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n__global__ void scale_kernel(float* __restrict__ out, const float* __restrict__ g, int n) {\\\\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\\\\n    if (i < n) out[i] *= g[0];\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\ninline cudaError_t ix_swiglu(const float* gate, const float* up, float* out, int n, cudaStream_t s = 0) {\\\\n    if (n <= 0) return cudaErrorInvalidValue;\\\\n    swiglu_kernel<<<(n + 127) / 128, 128, 0, s>>>(gate, up, out, n);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n}\\\\ninline cudaError_t ix_scalar_gate(const uint16_t* x_bf16, const uint16_t* w_bf16, float* out, int n_embd,\\\\n                                  cudaStream_t s = 0) {\\\\n    if (n_embd <= 0) return cudaErrorInvalidValue;\\\\n    scalar_gate_kernel<<<1, GATE_THREADS, 0, s>>>(x_bf16, w_bf16, out, n_embd);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n}\\\\ninline cudaError_t ix_scale(float* out, const float* g, int n, cudaStream_t s = 0) {\\\\n    if (n <= 0) return cudaErrorInvalidValue;\\\\n    scale_kernel<<<(n + 127) / 128, 128, 0, s>>>(out, g, n);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n}\\\\n}  // namespace ix_se\\\\n// ix_moe_combine.cu - MoE block final combination: y[j] = sum_e w[e]*parts[e][j] (+ shared[j], added plain).\\\\n// Port of Ref src/kernels/cuda/shared_expert.cu moe_combine_kernel (HEAD 46c152a1, L146, matches v9).\\\\n// No warp/wave ops, no ivcore10 change needed: kernel body is verbatim. Double accumulator, ascending e (matches reference).\\\\n// STATUS: NOT yet compiled or run on BI-V100.\\\\n#pragma once\\\\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\\\\n#include <cstddef>\\\\n\\\\n__global__ void moe_combine_kernel(const float* __restrict__ parts, const float* __restrict__ weights,\\\\n                                   const float* __restrict__ shared, float* __restrict__ y, int n_embd,\\\\n                                   int k, int has_shared) {\\\\n    const int j = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\\\\n    if (j >= n_embd) return;\\\\n    // Accumulate in DOUBLE, in the reference's own order (`for i in range(k): out[t] += w[t,i] * g[0]`).\\\\n    // f32 would be defensible for ten terms, but the reference is float64 and this is one line.\\\\n    double acc = 0.0;\\\\n    for (int e = 0; e < k; ++e) acc += (double) weights[e] * (double) parts[(size_t) e * n_embd + j];\\\\n    // The SHARED output is added PLAIN - not router-weighted, not renormalised against the routed sum.\\\\n    if (has_shared) acc += (double) shared[j];\\\\n    y[j] = (float) acc;\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// Single token: parts [k, n_embd], weights [k], shared [n_embd] (ignored if has_shared==0), y [n_embd].\\\\ninline cudaError_t ix_moe_combine(const float* parts, const float* weights, const float* shared, float* y,\\\\n                                  int n_embd, int k, int has_shared, cudaStream_t s = 0) {\\\\n    if (n_embd <= 0 || k <= 0) return cudaErrorInvalidValue;\\\\n    const int TH = 256;\\\\n    moe_combine_kernel<<<(n_embd + TH - 1) / TH, TH, 0, s>>>(parts, weights, shared, y, n_embd, k, has_shared);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n}\\\\n\\\",\\\"stderr\\\":\\\"\\\"}\\n$ cd /home/claude/baloon && cat src/ix_gdn.cu | head -140; sed -n 1,95p src/ix_router_top10_wave.cu; cat src/rt/kernels.cu; cat include/baloon/kern_cuda_misc.hpp | head -115\\n{\\\"returncode\\\":0,\\\"stdout\\\":\\\"// ix_gdn.cu - Phase 1 #6: gated delta-net non-projection kernels for ivcore10 (wave64).\\\\n// Port of Ref src/kernels/cuda/gdn.cu (HEAD 46c152a1): gdn_step, gdn_conv, gdn_l2_norm, gdn_beta_gate, gdn_out_norm.\\\\n// ivcore10 changes (v9 Part H):\\\\n//   * l2_kernel and out_norm: one WAVE (64 lanes) per row/head -> block = 64 threads, shfl_down off=32..1 width 64.\\\\n//     (Launching 32 threads with a width-64 shuffle would read inactive lanes - blockDim MUST be 64.)\\\\n//     The reduction stays DOUBLE; its summation order changes (lane stride 64), so results differ from Ref only at ~1e-16.\\\\n//   * step: JTHREADS 32 -> 64 (one thread per (h,j) state column; per-column arithmetic is untouched, so the recurrence is\\\\n//     op-for-op identical to Ref - only the block packing changes).\\\\n//   * conv, beta_gate: no warp ops, verbatim.\\\\n// STATUS: NOT yet compiled or run on BI-V100.\\\\n#pragma once\\\\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\\\\n#include <cmath>\\\\n#include <cstddef>\\\\n#include \\\\\\\"ix_compat.h\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nnamespace ix_gdn {\\\\nconstexpr int JTHREADS = 64;   // threads along j (state fast axis); S=128 -> 2 blocks per head\\\\nconstexpr int MAX_H = 1;       // heads per block (latency-hiding knob, see Ref comment); retune once SM count is known\\\\nconstexpr int WAVE = IX_WARP_SIZE;\\\\n\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ float softplus_f(float x) { return x > 20.0f ? x : log1pf(expf(x)); }\\\\n__device__ __forceinline__ float sigmoid_f(float x) { return 1.0f / (1.0f + expf(-x)); }\\\\n\\\\n__global__ void gdn_step_kernel(float* __restrict__ state, const float* __restrict__ q,\\\\n                                const float* __restrict__ k, const float* __restrict__ v,\\\\n                                const float* __restrict__ gate, const float* __restrict__ beta,\\\\n                                float* __restrict__ o, int S, int h_k, int h_v) {\\\\n    __shared__ float ks[MAX_H][128];\\\\n    __shared__ float qs[MAX_H][128];\\\\n    __shared__ float dec_s[MAX_H];\\\\n    __shared__ float beta_s[MAX_H];\\\\n    const int h0 = blockIdx.y * MAX_H;\\\\n    const int nh = min(MAX_H, h_v - h0);\\\\n    const int j = blockIdx.x * JTHREADS + threadIdx.x;\\\\n    for (int hi = 0; hi < nh; ++hi) {\\\\n        const int h = h0 + hi;\\\\n        const int src = h % h_k;                       // MODULO head pairing\\\\n        for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < S; i += JTHREADS) { ks[hi][i] = k[src * S + i]; qs[hi][i] = q[src * S + i]; }\\\\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) { dec_s[hi] = expf(gate[h]); beta_s[hi] = beta[h]; }\\\\n    }\\\\n    __syncthreads();\\\\n    if (j >= S) return;\\\\n    for (int hi = 0; hi < nh; ++hi) {\\\\n        const int h = h0 + hi;\\\\n        const float dec = dec_s[hi], b = beta_s[hi];\\\\n        float* col = state + (size_t) h * S + j;        // (S, h_v, S), j fastest\\\\n        const size_t stride = (size_t) h_v * S;\\\\n        float sk = 0.0f;                                // pass 1: decay BEFORE the update (PROPERTY 5), contract with k\\\\n        for (int i = 0; i < S; ++i) { const float s = col[(size_t) i * stride] * dec; col[(size_t) i * stride] = s; sk += s * ks[hi][i]; }\\\\n        const float d = (v[(size_t) h * S + j] - sk) * b;\\\\n        float dot = 0.0f;                               // pass 2: rank-1 update, read out against q with the UPDATED state\\\\n        for (int i = 0; i < S; ++i) { const float s = col[(size_t) i * stride] + ks[hi][i] * d; col[(size_t) i * stride] = s; dot += s * qs[hi][i]; }\\\\n        o[(size_t) h * S + j] = dot;\\\\n    }\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n__global__ void gdn_conv_kernel(float* __restrict__ cs, const float* __restrict__ x, const float* __restrict__ kW,\\\\n                                float* __restrict__ out, int C, int dc) {\\\\n    const int c = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\\\\n    if (c >= C) return;\\\\n    float* st = cs + (size_t) c * (dc - 1);\\\\n    const float* w = kW + (size_t) c * dc;\\\\n    float acc = 0.0f;\\\\n    for (int i = 0; i < dc - 1; ++i) acc += st[i] * w[i];\\\\n    acc += x[c] * w[dc - 1];\\\\n    out[c] = acc;\\\\n    for (int i = 0; i < dc - 2; ++i) st[i] = st[i + 1];\\\\n    st[dc - 2] = x[c];\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// One wave per row; launch <<<rows, 64>>>. Only lane 0 holds the full sum after the shfl_down tree.\\\\n__global__ void gdn_l2_kernel(float* __restrict__ x, int cols, float eps) {\\\\n    float* p = x + (size_t) blockIdx.x * cols;\\\\n    double acc = 0.0;\\\\n    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < cols; i += blockDim.x) acc += (double) p[i] * (double) p[i];\\\\n#pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int off = 32; off > 0; off >>= 1) acc += __shfl_down(acc, off, 64);\\\\n    __shared__ double ssum;\\\\n    if (threadIdx.x == 0) ssum = acc;\\\\n    __syncthreads();\\\\n    const float inv = (float) (1.0 / sqrt(ssum + (double) eps));\\\\n    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < cols; i += blockDim.x) p[i] *= inv;\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// y = rms_norm(o, eps) * ssm_norm * sigmoid(z), one wave per head; launch <<<h_v, 64>>>.\\\\n__global__ void gdn_out_norm_kernel(const float* __restrict__ o, const float* __restrict__ z,\\\\n                                    const float* __restrict__ ssm_norm, float* __restrict__ y, int S, float eps) {\\\\n    const int h = blockIdx.x;\\\\n    const float* po = o + (size_t) h * S; const float* pz = z + (size_t) h * S; float* py = y + (size_t) h * S;\\\\n    double acc = 0.0;\\\\n    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < S; i += blockDim.x) acc += (double) po[i] * (double) po[i];\\\\n#pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int off = 32; off > 0; off >>= 1) acc += __shfl_down(acc, off, 64);\\\\n    __shared__ double ssum;\\\\n    if (threadIdx.x == 0) ssum = acc;\\\\n    __syncthreads();\\\\n    const float inv = (float) (1.0 / sqrt(ssum / (double) S + (double) eps));\\\\n    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < S; i += blockDim.x) py[i] = po[i] * inv * ssm_norm[i] * sigmoid_f(pz[i]);\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n__global__ void gdn_beta_gate_kernel(float* __restrict__ beta, int n) {\\\\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\\\\n    if (i < n) beta[i] = sigmoid_f(beta[i]);\\\\n}\\\\n}  // namespace ix_gdn\\\\n\\\\ninline cudaError_t ix_gdn_step(float* state, const float* q, const float* k, const float* v, const float* gate,\\\\n                               const float* beta, float* o, int S, int h_k, int h_v, cudaStream_t s = 0) {\\\\n    if (S <= 0 || h_k <= 0 || h_v <= 0 || S > 128) return cudaErrorInvalidValue;   // ks/qs staged as [MAX_H][128]\\\\n    const dim3 grid((unsigned) ((S + ix_gdn::JTHREADS - 1) / ix_gdn::JTHREADS), (unsigned) ((h_v + ix_gdn::MAX_H - 1) / ix_gdn::MAX_H));\\\\n    ix_gdn::gdn_step_kernel<<<grid, ix_gdn::JTHREADS, 0, s>>>(state, q, k, v, gate, beta, o, S, h_k, h_v);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n}\\\\ninline cudaError_t ix_gdn_conv_step(float* conv_state, const float* x, const float* kW, float* out, int channels,\\\\n                                    int d_conv, cudaStream_t s = 0) {\\\\n    if (channels <= 0 || d_conv < 2) return cudaErrorInvalidValue;   // d_conv==1 would index st[-1] in Ref too\\\\n    ix_gdn::gdn_conv_kernel<<<(channels + 255) / 256, 256, 0, s>>>(conv_state, x, kW, out, channels, d_conv);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n}\\\\ninline cudaError_t ix_gdn_l2_norm(float* x, int rows, int cols, float eps, cudaStream_t s = 0) {\\\\n    if (rows <= 0 || cols <= 0 || cols > 1024) return cudaErrorInvalidValue;\\\\n    ix_gdn::gdn_l2_kernel<<<rows, 64, 0, s>>>(x, cols, eps);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n}\\\\ninline cudaError_t ix_gdn_beta_gate(float* beta, int h_v, cudaStream_t s = 0) {\\\\n    if (beta == nullptr || h_v <= 0) return cudaErrorInvalidValue;\\\\n    ix_gdn::gdn_beta_gate_kernel<<<(h_v + 127) / 128, 128, 0, s>>>(beta, h_v);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n}\\\\ninline cudaError_t ix_gdn_out_norm(const float* o, const float* z, const float* ssm_norm, float* y, int h_v, int S,\\\\n                                   float eps, cudaStream_t s = 0) {\\\\n    if (h_v <= 0 || S <= 0) return cudaErrorInvalidValue;\\\\n    ix_gdn::gdn_out_norm_kernel<<<h_v, 64, 0, s>>>(o, z, ssm_norm, y, S, eps);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n}\\\\n// ix_router_top10_wave.cu - MoE router (softmax / top-k / renormalise), one wavefront (64 lanes) per token.\\\\n// Port of Ref src/kernels/cuda/router_top10.cu router_top10_wave_kernel (HEAD 46c152a1, L190-263).\\\\n// ivcore10 change: guard REF_HIP_GFX906 -> __ILUVATAR__. Kernel body is verbatim (already wave64).\\\\n// STATUS: NOT yet compiled or run on BI-V100 (no corex toolchain on the dev box).\\\\n#pragma once\\\\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\\\\n#include <cmath>\\\\n#include <cstddef>\\\\n\\\\n#if defined(__ILUVATAR__)\\\\n// AMD (wave64): the same routing in ONE wavefront per token.  The block-wide kernel above spends its time in\\\\n// barriers - 10 selection passes with two __syncthreads each over 8 logical warps - not in arithmetic (43 us per\\\\n// token on gfx906).  Here every lane holds up to RW_PER probabilities in registers and each pass is a 64-lane\\\\n// butterfly argmax: no LDS round trip, no block barrier.  Bit-identical to the kernel above: fmaxf trees are\\\\n// exact, the exponentials and the (float) products are the same expressions, the sum stays one serial ascending\\\\n// scan, and the argmax keeps the lowest index on a tie (a total order, so the butterfly's association is free).\\\\nconstexpr int RW_PER = 8;   // n_expert <= 64 * 8\\\\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(64) router_top10_wave_kernel(const float* __restrict__ logits, int n_tokens,\\\\n                                                               int n_expert, int k, int* __restrict__ ids,\\\\n                                                               float* __restrict__ weights) {\\\\n    __shared__ double s_ex[64 * RW_PER];\\\\n    __shared__ double s_sum;\\\\n    const int t = blockIdx.x;\\\\n    if (t >= n_tokens) return;\\\\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\\\\n    const float* l = logits + (size_t) t * n_expert;\\\\n    float lv[RW_PER];\\\\n    float mx = -INFINITY;\\\\n#pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int j = 0; j < RW_PER; ++j) {\\\\n        const int e = lane + 64 * j;\\\\n        lv[j] = e < n_expert ? l[e] : -INFINITY;\\\\n        mx = fmaxf(mx, lv[j]);\\\\n    }\\\\n#pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int off = 32; off > 0; off >>= 1) mx = fmaxf(mx, __shfl_xor(mx, off, 64));\\\\n#pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int j = 0; j < RW_PER; ++j) {\\\\n        const int e = lane + 64 * j;\\\\n        if (e < n_expert) s_ex[e] = exp((double) lv[j] - (double) mx);\\\\n    }\\\\n    __syncthreads();\\\\n    if (lane == 0) {\\\\n        double sum = 0.0;\\\\n        for (int e = 0; e < n_expert; ++e) sum += s_ex[e];\\\\n        s_sum = sum;\\\\n    }\\\\n    __syncthreads();\\\\n    const float inv = (float) (1.0 / s_sum);\\\\n    float p[RW_PER];\\\\n#pragma unroll\\\\n    for (int j = 0; j < RW_PER; ++j) {\\\\n        const int e = lane + 64 * j;\\\\n        p[j] = e < n_expert ? (float) (s_ex[e] * inv) : -INFINITY;\\\\n    }\\\\n    unsigned taken = 0;\\\\n    for (int i = 0; i < k; ++i) {\\\\n        float bv = -INFINITY;\\\\n        int bi = n_expert;\\\\n#pragma unroll\\\\n        for (int j = 0; j < RW_PER; ++j) {\\\\n            const int e = lane + 64 * j;\\\\n            if (e >= n_expert || (taken >> j & 1u)) continue;\\\\n            if (p[j] > bv || (p[j] == bv && e < bi)) { bv = p[j]; bi = e; }\\\\n        }\\\\n#pragma unroll\\\\n        for (int off = 32; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\\\\n            const float ov = __shfl_xor(bv, off, 64);\\\\n            const int oi = __shfl_xor(bi, off, 64);\\\\n            if (ov > bv || (ov == bv && oi < bi)) { bv = ov; bi = oi; }\\\\n        }\\\\n        if (bi >= n_expert) break;\\\\n        if ((bi & 63) == lane) taken |= 1u << (bi >> 6);\\\\n        if (lane == 0) { ids[(size_t) t * k + i] = bi; weights[(size_t) t * k + i] = bv; }\\\\n    }\\\\n    if (lane == 0) {\\\\n        double s = 0.0;\\\\n        for (int i = 0; i < k; ++i) s += (double) weights[(size_t) t * k + i];\\\\n        const double sc = fmax(s, 6.103515625e-05);       // 2**-14\\\\n        for (int i = 0; i < k; ++i)\\\\n            weights[(size_t) t * k + i] = (float) ((double) weights[(size_t) t * k + i] / sc);\\\\n    }\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n// Host launcher: logits [n_tokens, n_expert] f32 -> ids/weights [n_tokens, k]. Requires n_expert <= 64*RW_PER (512).\\\\ninline cudaError_t ix_router_top10_wave(const float* logits, int n_tokens, int n_expert, int k,\\\\n                                        int* ids, float* weights, cudaStream_t s = 0) {\\\\n    if (n_expert > 64 * RW_PER || n_expert <= 0 || k <= 0 || k > n_expert) return cudaErrorInvalidValue;\\\\n    router_top10_wave_kernel<<<n_tokens, 64, 0, s>>>(logits, n_tokens, n_expert, k, ids, weights);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError();\\\\n}\\\\n#endif  // __ILUVATAR__\\\\n// kernels.cu - bf16 gemv and embedding gather kernels.\\\\n#include \\\\\\\"baloon/rt_kernels.hpp\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nnamespace baloon {\\\\nnamespace rt {\\\\n\\\\n__global__ void bf16_gemv_kernel(const uint16_t* w, const float* x, int64_t cols, float* out) {\\\\n    __shared__ float part[256];\\\\n    const uint16_t* row = w + (int64_t)blockIdx.x * cols;\\\\n    float acc = 0.f;\\\\n    for (int64_t c = threadIdx.x; c < cols; c += blockDim.x) {\\\\n        union { uint32_t u; float f; } cv; cv.u = (uint32_t)row[c] << 16;\\\\n        acc += cv.f * x[c];\\\\n    }\\\\n    part[threadIdx.x] = acc;\\\\n    __syncthreads();\\\\n    for (unsigned s = blockDim.x / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) {\\\\n        if (threadIdx.x < s) part[threadIdx.x] += part[threadIdx.x + s];\\\\n        __syncthreads();\\\\n    }\\\\n    if (threadIdx.x == 0) out[blockIdx.x] = part[0];\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n__global__ void embed_gather_kernel(const float* table, int64_t vocab, int64_t n, const int32_t* tokens, int64_t count, float* out) {\\\\n    const int64_t row = blockIdx.x;\\\\n    if (row >= count) return;\\\\n    const int32_t t = tokens[row];\\\\n    const bool valid = t >= 0 && (int64_t)t < vocab;\\\\n    for (int64_t c = threadIdx.x; c < n; c += blockDim.x) out[row * n + c] = valid ? table[(int64_t)t * n + c] : 0.f;\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nbool bf16_gemv(const uint16_t* w, const float* x, int64_t rows, int64_t cols, float* out, cudaStream_t s) {\\\\n    if (rows <= 0 || cols <= 0 || rows > 2147483647ll || !w || !x || !out) return false;\\\\n    bf16_gemv_kernel<<<(unsigned)rows, 256, 0, s>>>(w, x, cols, out);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError() == cudaSuccess;\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nbool embed_gather(const float* table, int64_t vocab, int64_t n, const int32_t* tokens, int64_t count, float* out, cudaStream_t s) {\\\\n    if (vocab <= 0 || n <= 0 || count < 0 || count > 2147483647ll || !table) return false;\\\\n    if (count == 0) return true;\\\\n    if (!tokens || !out) return false;\\\\n    embed_gather_kernel<<<(unsigned)count, 256, 0, s>>>(table, vocab, n, tokens, count, out);\\\\n    return cudaGetLastError() == cudaSuccess;\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n}  // namespace rt\\\\n}  // namespace baloon\\\\n// kern_cuda_misc.hpp - public launchers of the unit-14 CUDA sources: dequantization to bf16/f16/f32, S-family GEMV,\\\\n// Q2_0 grouped expert (S2) kernels, sampler. Host-side declarations only (include <cuda_runtime.h> first).\\\\n#pragma once\\\\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\\\\n\\\\n#include <cstddef>\\\\n#include <cstdint>\\\\n\\\\n#include \\\\\\\"baloon/kern_geometry.hpp\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nnamespace baloon {\\\\nnamespace kern {\\\\n\\\\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- dequant_bf16.cu\\\\n// Types: 0 F32, 1 F16, 30 BF16 (plain conversion) and the ggml/engine quantized types\\\\n// 2 Q4_0, 6 Q5_0, 8 Q8_0, 20 IQ4_NL, 11 Q3_K, 12 Q4_K, 13 Q5_K, 14 Q6_K, 23 IQ4_XS, 42 Q2_0.\\\\n// n is the element count (a whole number of blocks for the quantized types). dst may be uint16_t bits (bf16/f16) or float.\\\\nbool dequant_bf16_supported(int type);\\\\ncudaError_t dequant_bf16(int type, const void* src, uint16_t* dst, int64_t n, cudaStream_t stream);\\\\ncudaError_t dequant_f16(int type, const void* src, uint16_t* dst, int64_t n, cudaStream_t stream);\\\\ncudaError_t dequant_f32(int type, const void* src, float* dst, int64_t n, cudaStream_t stream);\\\\n\\\\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- s_gemv.cu\\\\n// y[row] = sum_i w[row][i] * x[i], w = decode(code) * group scale. bits = 2 (code-1), 4 (IQ4_NL codebook), 8 (int8).\\\\n// codes: row-major, n_in*bits/8 bytes per row. scales: fp16 bits, n_in >> group_shift per row. group = 1 << group_shift\\\\n// elements (16, 32 or 64). n_in % 16 == 0.\\\\ncudaError_t s_gemv(int bits, const uint8_t* codes, const uint16_t* scales, const float* x, float* y, int n_in, int n_out,\\\\n                   int group_shift, cudaStream_t stream);\\\\n// Activation as Q8_K blocks (292 bytes / 256 elements); n_in % 256 == 0. One warp per row.\\\\ncudaError_t s_gemv_q8k(int bits, const uint8_t* codes, const uint16_t* scales, const uint8_t* x_q8k, float* y, int n_in,\\\\n                       int n_out, int group_shift, cudaStream_t stream);\\\\n// Split-K: the lane iterations are cut into `splits` ranges whose partial sums land in `partials` (splits * n_out floats)\\\\n// and are added in range order by a finishing kernel (deterministic).\\\\ncudaError_t s_gemv_q8k_split(int bits, const uint8_t* codes, const uint16_t* scales, const uint8_t* x_q8k, float* y,\\\\n                             float* partials, int n_in, int n_out, int group_shift, int splits, cudaStream_t stream);\\\\n// Activation as Q8_0 blocks (34 bytes / 32 elements); n_in % 32 == 0.\\\\ncudaError_t s_gemv_q8_0_split(int bits, const uint8_t* codes, const uint16_t* scales, const uint8_t* x_q8_0, float* y,\\\\n                              float* partials, int n_in, int n_out, int group_shift, int splits, cudaStream_t stream);\\\\n// Dispatch by activation form: q8k != 0 -> Q8_K, otherwise Q8_0. The _async form never synchronizes.\\\\ncudaError_t s_gemv_split(int bits, const uint8_t* codes, const uint16_t* scales, const uint8_t* x_q, int q8k, float* y,\\\\n                         float* partials, int n_in, int n_out, int group_shift, int splits, cudaStream_t stream);\\\\ncudaError_t s_gemv_split_async(int bits, const uint8_t* codes, const uint16_t* scales, const uint8_t* x_q, int q8k, float* y,\\\\n                               float* partials, int n_in, int n_out, int group_shift, int splits, cudaStream_t stream);\\\\nbool q8k_form_ok(int n_in);\\\\n\\\\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- s2_expert_grouped.cu\\\\n// Resident Q2_0 experts: blob of slot s at pool + s * slot_bytes (slot_bytes >= q2geo::BLOB). All launchers are\\\\n// asynchronous on `stream`; `scratch` must hold moe_hit_grouped_scratch_bytes(n_tok, K) bytes.\\\\nsize_t moe_hit_grouped_scratch_bytes(int n_tok, int K);\\\\n\\\\n// Resident-hit selection by ballot-prefix compaction. ids: n_tok*K selected experts; slot_of_expert: NE entries (-1 = not\\\\n// resident). row, the 8 warps splitting the reduction.\\\\n* Gate: one warp per output row, 1280 blocks of 8 warps cover 10240 rows; each lane does hc_lr/32 = 10 terms.\\\\n* Write: out[i] = R[i] + block_out[i % n_embd] * w[i / n_embd] (every stream adds the same block output).\\\\n* Workspace: five regions each rounded up to 16 bytes, laid out consecutively without overlap.\\\\n* Fused up: block covers 32 columns x 4 streams = 128 rows; a (block, row) pair addresses exactly one of 10240 entries.\\\\n\\\\n## Form template identifiers\\\\nN -> 2560; HC -> 4; D -> 10240; LR -> 320; WARPS -> 8.\\\\n-/\\\\n\\\\nnamespace GatedResidual\\\\n\\\\n-- § helpers\\\\n\\\\n/-- Row-major index bound. -/\\\\ntheorem idx2_lt (a b A B : Nat) (ha : a < A) (hb : b < B) : a*B+b < A*B := by\\\\n  have h1 : (a+1)*B ≤ A*B := Nat.mul_le_mul_right B (by omega)\\\\n  rw [Nat.add_mul, Nat.one_mul] at h1\\\\n  omega\\\\n\\\\n-- § geometry constants\\\\n\\\\n/-- `hc * n_embd = 10240`. -/\\\\ntheorem hc_dim_val : 2560 * 4 = 10240 := by decide\\\\n\\\\n/-- Down projection: 40 blocks of 8 warps are exactly the 320 rows. -/\\\\ntheorem down_blocks_exact : 320 / 8 = 40 ∧ 40 * 8 = 320 := by decide\\\\n\\\\n/-- The up kernel's 80 blocks of 32 columns are exactly n_embd; the multi variant's 160 x 16 as well. -/\\\\ntheorem up_blocks_exact : 80 * 32 = 2560 ∧ 160 * 16 = 2560 := by decide\\\\n\\\\n/-- 320 hidden values are 40 chunks of 8: 32 lanes take one, 8 lanes take a second. -/\\\\ntheorem lr_chunks : 320 / 8 = 40 ∧ 40 - 32 = 8 := by decide\\\\n\\\\n/-- One warp covers a 10240-row reduction as 1280 chunks, 40 per lane. -/\\\\ntheorem down_chunks_per_lane : 10240 / 8 = 1280 ∧ 1280 / 32 = 40 := by decide\\\\n\\\\n/-- Fused multi-token tiling: 4 tiles of 2560, each 320 chunks = 10 per lane. -/\\\\ntheorem tiling : 10240 / 2560 = 4 ∧ 2560 / 8 / 32 = 10 := by decide\\\\n\\\\n-- § work distribution\\\\n\\\\n/-- Down rows: every row `k < 320` is (block, warp) = (k/8, k%8). -/\\\\ntheorem down_row_cover (k : Nat) (h : k < 320) : k / 8 < 40 ∧ k = (k / 8) * 8 + k % 8 ∧ k % 8 < 8 := by omega\\\\n\\\\n/-- Block-wide split of the 10240-element reduction among 8 warps x 32 lanes: each element has one owner. -/\\\\ntheorem reduction_owner (i : Nat) (h : i < 10240) :\\\\n    i = (i / 256) * 256 + ((i % 256) / 32) * 32 + i % 32 ∧ (i % 256) / 32 < 8 ∧ i % 32 < 32 := by omega\\\\n\\\\n/-- Gate rows: 1280 blocks of 8 warps cover 10240 rows. -/\\\\ntheorem gate_row_cover (i : Nat) (h : i < 10240) : i / 8 < (10240 + 7) / 8 ∧ i = (i / 8) * 8 + i % 8 := by omega\\\\n\\\\n/-- Hidden-vector lane coverage inside a 320-wide reduction. -/\\\\ntheorem lane_cover_320 (j : Nat) (h : j < 320) : j = (j / 32) * 32 + j % 32 ∧ j / 32 < 10 := by omega\\\\n\\\\n/-- Tile chunk coverage: each of the 320 chunks per tile is owned by one (lane, q). -/\\\\ntheorem tile_chunk_cover (j : Nat) (h : j < 320) : j = j % 32 + 32 * (j / 32) ∧ j % 32 < 32 ∧ j / 32 < 10 := by\\\\n  omega\\\\n\\\\n-- § up kernel addressing\\\\n\\\\n/-- Fused up kernel: (block, row r in 0..127) addresses stream `r/32`, column `blk*32 + r%32`; index in range. -/\\\\ntheorem up_index_lt (blk r : Nat) (hb : blk < 80) (hr : r < 128) :\\\\n    (r / 32) * 2560 + blk * 32 + r % 32 < 10240 := by omega\\\\n\\\\n/-- Every entry of the 10240-wide residual is produced by exactly one (block, row) pair of the up kernel. -/\\\\ntheorem up_index_surjective (i : Nat) (h : i < 10240) :\\\\n    ∃ blk r, blk < 80 ∧ r < 128 ∧ i = (r / 32) * 2560 + blk * 32 + r % 32 := by\\\\n  refine ⟨(i % 2560) / 32, (i / 2560) * 32 + (i % 2560) % 32, by omega, by omega, by omega⟩\\\\n\\\\n/-- ...and injective. -/\\\\ntheorem up_index_injective (b1 b2 r1 r2 : Nat) (hb1 : b1 < 80) (hb2 : b2 < 80) (hr1 : r1 < 128) (hr2 : r2 < 128)\\\\n    (h : (r1 / 32) * 2560 + b1 * 32 + r1 % 32 = (r2 / 32) * 2560 + b2 * 32 + r2 % 32) :\\\\n    b1 = b2 ∧ r1 = r2 := by omega\\\\n\\\\n-- § residual write\\\\n\\\\n/-- `out[i]` uses block output element `i % n` and stream weight `i / n`; both in range. -/\\\\ntheorem write_index_decode (i n hc : Nat) (hn : 0 < n) (h : i < hc * n) :\\\\n    i / n < hc ∧ i % n < n ∧ (i / n) * n + i % n = i := by\\\\n  refine ⟨(Nat.div_lt_iff_lt_mul hn).2 h, Nat.mod_lt _ hn, ?_⟩\\\\n  have := Nat.div_add_mod i n\\\\n  rw [Nat.mul_comm n (i / n)] at this\\\\n  exact this\\\\n\\\\n/-- Mean over streams reads `gated[c*n + d]` with c < hc, d < n: in range. -/\\\\ntheorem mean_index_lt (c d hc n : Nat) (hc' : c < hc) (hd : d < n) : c * n + d < hc * n :=\\\\n  idx2_lt c d hc n hc' hd\\\\n\\\\n-- § workspace layout\\\\n\\\\n/-- Round a byte size up to 16. -/\\\\ndef align16 (s : Nat) : Nat := (s + 15) / 16 * 16\\\\n\\\\n/-- Alignment never shrinks, adds less than 16, and yields a multiple of 16. -/\\\\ntheorem align16_props (s : Nat) : s ≤ align16 s ∧ align16 s < s + 16 ∧ align16 s % 16 = 0 := by\\\\n  unfold align16; omega\\\\n\\\\n/-- Consecutive regions do not overlap: next offset >= previous offset + previous size. -/\\\\ntheorem region_disjoint (off sz : Nat) : off + sz ≤ off + align16 sz := by\\\\n  have := (align16_props sz).1\\\\n  omega\\\\n\\\\n/-- Real geometry: xn + xq + lq + gated + lo = 104320 bytes. -/\\\\ntheorem workspace_total :\\\\n    align16 (10240 * 4) + align16 (10240 * 2) + align16 (320 * 2) + align16 (10240 * 4) + align16 (320 * 4)\\\\n      = 104320 := by decide\\\\n\\\\n/-- The original bug: giving `xq` 640 bytes where 20480 are needed is strictly too small. -/\\\\ntheorem xq_size_needed : 640 < 10240 * 2 := by decide\\\\n\\\\n/-- Block-sum scratch: 8 floats, and a double scratch of 8 is at least as large. -/\\\\ntheorem scratch_cast_safe : 8 * 4 ≤ 8 * 8 := by decide\\\\n\\\\nend Counts: 23 kernel prologues use `lane = t & 31, warp = t >> 5`; only 1 place uses `& 63`.\\\\n- Genuinely wave64: `gr_down_split_kernel` (L1641, `lane = t & 63`, under `#if REF_HIP_GFX906`, L1627-1701), `xor8` HIP branch\\\\n  (L1557, `__shfl_xor(v, o, 64)`), and the reduction at L1679.\\\\n- Everything else is written for 32-lane warps: `THREADS = 256`, `WARPS = THREADS / 32 = 8` (L42-43), `warp_sum` (L48-51), the q8_1 tails,\\\\n  `gr_quantize_q8_0_kernel` (L2469), the persistent HC read (L2861).\\\\n- Host guard L3075: `if (prop.warpSize != 32) ... REF_HC_PERSIST is off` - the persistent kernel refuses non-32 cards by design.\\\\n\\\\n## Why the 32-lane code can still work on wave64 (the QFUSE question)\\\\n`__shfl_xor(v, o)` with o in {16,8,4,2,1} flips only lane bits 0-4, so it never leaves the aligned 32-lane half of a 64-lane wave.\\\\nWith 256 threads, logical warp w = t>>5 is exactly half-wave (w&1) of hardware wave (w>>1). So the q8_1 tails\\\\n(`gr_q8_tail`-style, L246/L248/L687/L689/L2019/L2021/L2477) and `warp_sum` reduce each 32-value block inside one half-wave and give the\\\\nSAME butterfly sum as on NVIDIA - provided the shuffle is width-32 (or an xor <32) and nothing is wave-wide (no `__ballot`,\\\\n`__activemask`, `__syncwarp`: none found in the file). Early `return`s per logical warp (e.g. L240-243 `if (warp >= m.T) return;`)\\\\ntherefore diverge halves of a wave harmlessly.\\\\n\\\\n## What is NOT safe to copy verbatim (port rules for #7)\\\\n1. **L571** `__shfl_sync(0xffffffffu, tb, i * 8 + tj)` takes an ABSOLUTE source lane 0..31. Same for any other `__shfl(_sync)` with a computed source lane.\\\\n2. Replace every `__shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffffu, v, o)` with `ix_shfl_xor32(v, o)` / `ix_halfwave_reduce_*` (ix_compat.h). Do not rely on\\\\n   `_sync` variants existing on ivcore10 (ix_compat.h header comment: no `_sync` needed).\\\\n3. **`__nanosleep` guard L2801**: `#if !defined(__CUDA_ARCH__) || __CUDA_ARCH__ >= 700`. Change the guard to `#if !defined(__ILUVATAR__) && (...)`. `probe_halfwave` prints the value.\\\\n4. Persistent kernel (L2742-3080): drop the `warpSize != 32` host guard only after (1)-(3) are applied AND `probe_halfwave` passes;\\\\n   its barrier uses `clock64()` + atomics on `bar[]` - not yet checked for ivcore10 (`__threadfence()` only, no `_system`).\\\\n5. FILE: /mnt/user-data/outputs/ChatFrontend.lean\\n/-!\\n# MODULE: chat_frontend\\nSOURCES: serve/frontend.py\\nText-only request/response layer: normalizes OpenAI / Anthropic requests into chat-template form,\\nmaps reasoning effort / thinking budgets to template levels, and incrementally parses model output\\ninto reasoning, content and tool calls without ever emitting a partial tag.\\n\\n## Source semantics\\n* Thinking budgets map monotonically: `< 2048 -> low`, `< 8192 -> medium`, else `xhigh`.\\n* The parser holds back the longest suffix of the buffer that is a proper prefix of a tag, so a tag\\n  split across deltas is never shown; held + emitted text equals the buffer.\\n* Tool-call parameter values lose at most one leading and one trailing newline.\\n* One `<|image_pad|>` per image is expanded to one per image token; with no images the ids are unchanged.\\n* A request is rejected (never truncated) when prompt plus max tokens exceed the context.\\n\\n## Form template identifiers\\n* `budget_effort`            -> `budgetRank`\\n* `OutputParser._hold`       -> `holdOne`\\n* `parse_tool_call` strip    -> `stripNl`\\n* `Service.prepare` pad loop -> `expand`\\n-/\\n\\nnamespace Ref.Frontend\\n\\n-- § thinking budget -> level\\n\\n/-- Level rank of a thinking budget: 0 = low, 1 = medium, 2 = xhigh. -/\\ndef budgetRank (n : Int) : Nat := if n < 2048 then 0 else if n < 8192 then 1 else 2\\n\\n/-- More budget never yields a lower level. -/\\ntheorem budgetRank_mono (n m : Int) (h : n ≤ m) : budgetRank n ≤ budgetRank m := by\\n  unfold budgetRank\\n  split_ifs <;> omega\\n\\n/-- Levels are one of the three template values. -/\\ntheorem budgetRank_lt (n : Int) : budgetRank n < 3 := by\\n  unfold budgetRank\\n  split_ifs <;> omega\\n\\n-- § partial-tag hold-back\\n\\n/-- Longest `n` in `1 .. tag.length - 1` such that the last `n` symbols of `text` equal `tag.take n`. -/\\ndef holdOne (text tag : List Nat) : Nat :=\\n  (List.range tag.length).foldl\\n    (fun b n => if 1 ≤ n ∧ n ≤ text.length ∧ text.drop (text.length - n) = tag.take n then max b n else b) 0\\n\\n/-- Fold of `max`-style steps stays below a bound if every candidate is below it. -/\\ntheorem fold_bound (tl tl' : Nat) (f : Nat → Nat → Nat) (hf : ∀ b n, f b n ≤ max b n) :\\n    ∀ (l : List Nat) (b : Nat), (∀ n ∈ l, n < tl) → b < tl → l.foldl f b < tl := by\\n  intro l\\n  induction l with\\n  | nil => intro b _ hb; simpa using hb\\n  | cons n l ih =>\\n    intro b hl hb\\n    simp only [List.foldl_cons]\\n    apply ih\\n    · intro x hx\\n      exact hl x (List.mem_cons_of_mem _ hx)\\n    · have h1 := hf b n\\n      have h2 : n < tl := hl n (List.mem_cons_self)\\n      omega\\n\\n/-- The held suffix is a PROPER prefix of the tag: strictly shorter than the tag (for non-empty tags). -/\\ntheorem holdOne_lt (text tag : List Nat) (h : 0 < tag.length) : holdOne text tag < tag.length := by\\n  unfold holdOne\\n  apply fold_bound tag.length 0\\n  · intro b n\\n    split_ifs <;> omega\\n  · intro n hn\\n    exact List.mem_range.mp hn\\n  · exact h\\n\\n/-- Hold-back splits the buffer into an emitted prefix and a held suffix that recombine exactly. -/\\ntheorem hold_split (buf : List Nat) (keep : Nat) :\\n    buf.take (buf.length - keep) ++ buf.drop (buf.length - keep) = buf :=\\n  List.take_append_drop _ _\\n\\n/-- The emitted prefix is no longer than the buffer. -/\\ntheorem emitted_le (buf : List Nat) (keep : Nat) : (buf.take (buf.length - keep)).length ≤ buf.length := by\\n  simp [List.length_take]\\n\\n-- § newline stripping of parameter values\\n\\n/-- Remove one leading newline (code 10) if present. -/\\ndef dropLead : List Nat → List Nat\\n  | 10 :: rest => rest\\n  | l => l\\n\\n/-- Remove one trailing newline if present. -/\\ndef dropTrail (l : List Nat) : List Nat := if l.getLast? = some 10 then l.dropLast else l\\n\\n/-- Strip at most one newline at each end. -/\\ndef stripNl (l : List Nat) : List Nat := dropTrail (dropLead l)\\n\\n/-- Leading strip removes at most one symbol. -/\\ntheorem dropLead_length (l : List Nat) : l.length ≤ (dropLead l).length + 1 := by\\n  match l with\\n  | [] => simp [dropLead]\\n  | 10 :: rest => simp [dropLead]\\n  | (k + 11) :: rest => simp [dropLead]\\n  | 0 :: rest => simp [dropLead]\\n  | 1 :: rest => simp [dropLead]\\n  | 2 :: rest => simp [dropLead]\\n  | 3 :: rest => simp [dropLead]\\n  | 4 :: rest => simp [dropLead]\\n  | 5 :: rest => simp [dropLead]\\n  | 6 :: rest => simp [dropLead]\\n  | 7 :: rest => simp [dropLead]\\n  | 8 :: rest => simp [dropLead]\\n  | 9 :: rest => simp [dropLead]\\n\\n/-- Trailing strip removes at most one symbol. -/\\ntheorem dropTrail_length (l : List Nat) : l.length ≤ (dropTrail l).length + 1 := by\\n  unfold dropTrail\\n  split_ifs\\n  · simp [List.length_dropLast]\\n    omega\\n  · omega\\n\\n/-- Stripping never lengthens a value. -/\\ntheorem dropTrail_le (l : List Nat) : (dropTrail l).length ≤ l.length := by\\n  unfold dropTrail\\n  split_ifs\\n  · simp [List.length_dropLast]\\n  · exact Nat.le_refl _\\n\\n-- § image pad expansion\\n\\n/-- Expand each pad token into `c` pads using the per-image counts in order. -/\\ndef expand (pad : Nat) : List Nat → List Nat → List Nat\\n  | [], _ => []\\n  | t :: ts, cs =>\\n    if t = pad then\\n      match cs with\\n      | [] => t :: expand pad ts []\\n      | c :: cs' => List.replicate c pad ++ expand pad ts cs'\\n    else t :: expand pad ts cs\\n\\n/-- With no image counts the ids are unchanged. -/\\ntheorem expand_nil_counts (pad : Nat) : ∀ ids : List Nat, expand pad ids [] = ids := by\\n  intro ids\\n  induction ids with\\n  | nil => rfl\\n  | cons t ts ih =>\\n    by_cases h : t = pad <;> simp [expand, h, ih]\\n\\n/-- Ids without any pad token are unchanged for any counts. -/\\ntheorem expand_no_pad (pad : Nat) : ∀ (ids cs : List Nat), (∀ t ∈ ids, t ≠ pad) → expand pad ids cs = ids := by\\n  intro ids\\n  induction ids with\\n  | nil => intro cs _; rfl\\n  | cons t ts ih =>\\n    intro cs h\\n    have ht : t ≠ pad := h t (List.mem_cons_self)\\n    have h2 := ih cs (fun x hx => h x (List.mem_cons_of_mem _ hx))\\n    simp [expand, ht, h2]\\n\\n-- § context admission\\n\\n/-- A request is admitted iff prompt plus max tokens fit the engine context. -/\\ndef fits (promptLen maxNew maxCtx : Nat) : Prop := promptLen + maxNew ≤ maxCtx\\n\\n/-- An admitted request's prompt alone fits the context. -/\\ntheorem fits_prompt (p m c : Nat) (h : fits p m c) : p ≤ c := by\\n  unfold fits at h\\n  omega\\n\\n/-- Detokenizer: after pushing, `sent` equals the decoded length, so the next delta starts there. -/\\ndef deltaLen (textLen sent : Nat) : Nat := textLen - sent\\n\\n/-- Emitted deltas never overlap: sent + delta = text length when sent is not ahead. -/\\ntheorem delta_exact (textLen sent : Nat) (h : sent ≤ textLen) : sent + deltaLen textLen sent = textLen := by\\n  unfold deltaLen\\n  omega\\n\\nend Ref.Frontend\\n\\n\\n\\n======\\n## FILE: /mnt/user-data/outputs/ServeEngine.lean\\n/-!\\n# MODULE: serve_engine\\nSOURCES: serve/server.py\\nOpenAI / Anthropic HTTP front for a single resident engine: a FIFO-serialized `Service`, a\\n`RefEngine` speaking the `GEN / T / DONE / ERR` line protocol, an image-embedding cache\\nbounded to 64 entries, API-key authorization and finish-reason mapping.\\n\\n## Source semantics\\n* Requests are admitted only if prompt + max tokens fit the engine context (400 otherwise).\\n* The engine emits at most `max_new` `T` lines per request and finishes with exactly one `DONE`.\\n* The vision cache never holds more than 64 entries (oldest evicted first).\\n* With no API key configured every request is authorized; otherwise the supplied key must equal it.\\n* OpenAI finish is `tool_calls` iff at least one call was made and generation stopped naturally;\\n  Anthropic stop reason is `tool_use` under the same condition.\\n\\n## Form template identifiers\\n* `Service.prepare` context check -> `admit`\\n* `Vision.cache` eviction        -> `insertCache`\\n* `_authorized`                  -> `authorized`\\n* `openai_chunks` finish         -> `openaiFinish`\\n* `anthropic_events` stop        -> `anthropicStop`\\n-/\\n\\nnamespace Ref.Serve\\n\\n-- § admission\\n\\n/-- Prompt + max tokens must not exceed the engine context. -/\\ndef admit (promptLen maxNew maxCtx : Nat) : Bool := decide (promptLen + maxNew ≤ maxCtx)\\n\\n/-- An admitted request fits the context. -/\\ntheorem admit_sound (p m c : Nat) (h : admit p m c = true) : p + m ≤ c := by\\n  unfold admit at h\\n  exact of_decide_eq_true h\\n\\n/-- A request that does not fit is rejected, never truncated. -/\\ntheorem admit_reject (p m c : Nat) (h : c < p + m) : admit p m c = false := by\\n  unfold admit\\n  exact decide_eq_false (by omega)\\n\\n-- § engine line protocol\\n\\n/-- Server-side view of a request in flight: tokens streamed so far. -/\\nstructure Req where\\n  streamed : Nat\\n  maxNew : Nat\\n  done : Bool\\n\\n/-- Receiving a `T` line streams one token only while below `max_new`. -/\\ndef onToken (r : Req) : Req :=\\n  if r.done = false ∧ r.streamed < r.maxNew then { r with streamed := r.streamed + 1 } else r\\n\\n/-- `DONE` finishes the request. -/\\ndef onDone (r : Req) : Req := { r with done := true }\\n\\n/-- Token delivery never exceeds `max_new`. -/\\ntheorem onToken_le (r : Req) (h : r.streamed ≤ r.maxNew) : (onToken r).streamed ≤ r.maxNew := by\\n  unfold onToken\\n  split_ifs with hc\\n  · show r.streamed + 1 ≤ r.maxNew\\n    have := hc.2\\n    omega\\n  · exact h\\n\\n/-- After `DONE` no further token is delivered. -/\\ntheorem onToken_after_done (r : Req) : (onToken (onDone r)).streamed = r.streamed := by\\n  simp [onToken, onDone]\\n\\n/-- `DONE` is idempotent: a request finishes exactly once. -/\\ntheorem onDone_idem (r : Req) : onDone (onDone r) = onDone r := rfl\\n\\n-- § vision cache bound\\n\\n/-- Insert and evict the oldest when more than 64 entries. -/\\ndef insertCache (cache : List Nat) (x : Nat) : List Nat :=\\n  if 64 < cache.length + 1 then (cache ++ [x]).tail else cache ++ [x]\\n\\n/-- The cache never exceeds 64 entries (given it started within the bound). -/\\ntheorem insertCache_len (cache : List Nat) (x : Nat) (h : cache.length ≤ 64) :\\n    (insertCache cache x).length ≤ 64 := by\\n  unfold insertCache\\n  split_ifs with hc\\n  · rw [List.length_tail, List.length_append, List.length_singleton]\\n    omega\\n  · rw [List.length_append, List.length_singleton]\\n    omega\\n\\n-- § authorization\\n\\n/-- No key configured -> open; else the supplied key must match. -/\\ndef authorized (key gk : Nat) (configured : Bool) : Bool :=\\n  if configured = false then true else decide (gk = key)\\n\\n/-- With no API key every request is authorized. -/\\ntheorem authorized_open (key gk : Nat) : authorized key gk false = true := by\\n  simp [authorized]\\n\\n/-- With a key configured, only the matching key is authorized. -/\\ntheorem authorized_iff (key gk : Nat) : authorized key gk true = true ↔ gk = key := by\\n  simp [authorized]\\n\\n-- § finish reasons\\n\\n/-- Finish reasons exposed by the OpenAI side. -/\\ninductive Finish where\\n  | stop\\n  | length\\n  | toolCalls\\n  deriving DecidableEq\\n\\n/-- `tool_calls` iff at least one call and a natural stop; else `stop` / `length`. -/\\ndef openaiFinish (calls : Nat) (stopped : Bool) : Finish :=\\n  if stopped = true ∧ 0 < calls then Finish.toolCalls else if stopped = true then Finish.stop else Finish.length\\n\\n/-- The OpenAI `tool_calls` finish reason is exactly \\\"stopped naturally after calling a tool\\\". -/\\ntheorem openaiFinish_toolCalls_iff (c : Nat) (s : Bool) :\\n    openaiFinish c s = Finish.toolCalls ↔ (s = true ∧ 0 < c) := by\\n  cases s <;> rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos c with h | h <;> simp [openaiFinish, h]\\n\\n/-- Anthropic stop reasons. -/\\ninductive Stop where\\n  | endTurn\\n  | maxTokens\\n  | toolUse\\n  deriving DecidableEq\\n\\n/-- `tool_use` iff a tool block was emitted and generation stopped naturally. -/\\ndef anthropicStop (usedTool stopped : Bool) : Stop :=\\n  if usedTool = true ∧ stopped = true then Stop.toolUse else if stopped = true then Stop.endTurn else Stop.maxTokens\\n\\n/-- A length-limited generation never reports `tool_use`. -/\\ntheorem anthropicStop_length (u : Bool) : anthropicStop u false = Stop.maxTokens := by\\n  simp [anthropicStop]\\n\\n/-- A natural stop without tools is `end_turn`. -/\\ntheorem anthropicStop_end (u : Bool) (h : u = false) : anthropicStop u true = Stop.endTurn := by\\n  simp [anthropicStop, h]\\n\\n-- § usage accounting\\n\\n/-- OpenAI usage total = prompt + completion tokens. -/\\ndef totalTokens (prompt completion : Nat) : Nat := prompt + completion\\n\\n/-- The reported total is the sum of its parts. -/\\ntheorem totalTokens_eq (p c : Nat) : totalTokens p c = p + c := rfl\\n\\nend \\\"GEN <max_new> <tok> <tok> ...\\\" (at least one prompt token, max_new >= 1).\\ninline GenLine parse_gen(const std::string& line) {\\n    GenLine g;\\n    if (line.compare(0, 4, \\\"GEN \\\") != 0) return g;\\n    size_t i = 4;\\n    auto next = [&](long long& v) {\\n        while (i < line.size() && line[i] == ' ') ++i;\\n        if (i >= line.size()) return false;\\n        size_t j = i;\\n        bool neg = false;\\n        if (line[j] == '-') { neg = true; ++j; }\\n        if (j >= line.size() || line[j] < '0' || line[j] > '9') return false;\\n        long long x = 0;\\n        for (; j < line.size() && line[j] >= '0' && line[j] <= '9'; ++j) x = x * 10 + (line[j] - '0');\\n        if (j < line.size() && line[j] != ' ') return false;\\n        v = neg ? -x : x; i = j;\\n        return true;\\n    };\\n    long long m;\\n    if (!next(m) || m < 1) return g;\\n    g.max_new = (uint64_t)m;\\n    long long t;\\n    while (next(t)) g.tokens.push_back(t);\\n    while (i < line.size() && line[i] == ' ') ++i;\\n    if (i != line.size() || g.tokens.empty()) { g.tokens.clear(); g.max_new = 0; return g; }\\n    g.ok = true;\\n    return g;\\n}\\ninline std::string fmt_token(int64_t t) {
